- Live-tree rail: `project-harness` has no `aac-skills/` source — it lives in the owner's `~/.claude/skills`, which is read-only here. The template change was made in the `agents/skills/project-harness/` mirror and the payload rebuilt per CLAUDE.md (`python3 tools/skill-stamps.py stamp aac-skills agents/skills` then `python3 tools/build-cloud-plugin.py --from-mirror --home 'C:\Users\Dan'`). The owner must run `sync.ps1 -Mode pull` (or let the freshness loop auto-install) on the Windows machine after this merges; until then the live `~/.claude/skills/project-harness/templates/tracker-audit.js` is older than the mirror, and the next `sync.ps1 -Mode push` would overwrite the mirror and delete the new check from the template.
- Ambiguity resolved one way: the check reads only UNTICKED acceptance boxes. A ticked box naming a deleted path is finished work, and the ticket's own framing is "it cannot be ticked by doing the work". Ticked boxes are not scanned and no support for that reading was built.
- The harness version marker was bumped 19 -> 20 (`agents/skills/project-harness/SKILL.md` "Current version", `templates/harness-version.md`, and this repo's `docs/agents/harness-version.md`) with a v20 changelog row, because a template change that nothing bumps never reaches a harnessed repo and the previous commit on master was a fix for exactly that mismatch. Consequence outside this ticket: every other harnessed repo now reads as v19-behind, and their `/session-start` check STOPs until step 8 re-copies `tracker-audit.js` there.
- The new check found live tracker drift it was not written for: #173's acceptance box names `agents/skills/project-harness/templates/ticket-fleet.js`, which harness v18 deleted when the fleet became a plugin-served skill. That box cannot be ticked as written and the issue needs the box dropped or restated against `aac-skills/ticket-fleet/ticket-fleet.js`.
- Issue #361 itself now trips the advisory, because its own acceptance box quotes #120's `writing` box. That is correct behaviour (the box does name a deleted path) but it means a ticket ABOUT a deleted subject reports as one; if that reads as noise on future meta-tickets, the narrowing to add is a skip for boxes whose text names another issue number.
- Pre-existing and untouched: `node tools/tracker-audit.js` on this repo already reported one hard `ungated-dependency` finding before this change, so the live audit exits 1 rather than 0. Nothing in this change contributes a hard finding — `deleted-subject?` is advisory.
- The `.githooks/pre-commit` restore test could not run in this container: `core.hooksPath` is unset in the worktree and there is no `pwsh`/`powershell` on PATH, so the PowerShell restore suite (`tests/restore-test.ps1`) gating every commit never executed for this branch. It should run on the owner's machine before release.
- Verification gap on the PowerShell half: no PowerShell exists in this Linux container, and the worktree-isolation guard refuses to run a `pwsh` binary at all (it blocked `pwsh -NoProfile -File <parse-check>.ps1` as 'cannot be shown not to run git'), so /home/user/claude-dotfiles/.claude/worktrees/wf_29d1e857-86e-10/tests/settings-invariants.tests.ps1, sync.ps1 and tools/settings-invariants.ps1 were never executed or parsed here. The new insertion logic in Set-SettingsInvariant was instead ported line-for-line to Python and asserted byte-identical for the LF fixture, the CRLF fixture, an empty `"permissions": {}` block, an empty multi-line block and a minified one-line file; the rest is static review against the neighbouring suites' idioms. `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tests\restore-test.ps1` still needs one Windows run to close AC3.
- Ambiguity resolved one way: 'sync.ps1 reports failure' is implemented as fail-fast - Invoke-SettingsInvariants exits with the tool's own exit code at BOTH call sites (push, before the secret guard and the commit; pull, before Update-PersonalProfile and Write-DotfilesStamp). The pull case deliberately leaves ~/.claude/hook-state/dotfiles-sync/state.json unwritten so the freshness classifier cannot read a failed pull as a clean sync; the alternative reading (finish the pull, report non-zero at the end) was not built.
- Pre-existing bug, not fixed (already FOLLOW-UPS.md line 39): Restore-SkillLinks dies under Set-StrictMode when the repo carries no claude/skill-links.json - Read-JsonArray's `return @()` unrolls to $null and `$links.Count` throws - so a pull against a sandbox repo without that file cannot reach any later step. tests/settings-invariants.tests.ps1 works around it by copying the real claude/skill-links.json into its sandbox repo (every target resolves under the sandbox home and none exist, so all links are skipped).
- tools/settings-invariants.ps1 still throws (exit 1) for a defaultMode whose value is not a simple \"...\" string literal - e.g. `"defaultMode": null`. That is deliberate and unchanged, and it is the fixture the new pull-failure case uses to produce a non-zero exit; if a later ticket makes that path self-healing too, tests/settings-invariants.tests.ps1 case 3 needs a different way to force the tool to fail.
- The rewritten branch also now handles an EMPTY `"permissions": {}` block (insert with no trailing comma, closing brace moved onto its own line) because the same code path covers it. The ticket only names the 'other keys' case, so that sub-case is verified in the Python port only, not pinned by a test.
- Issue 363's stated mechanism does not reproduce against the code at HEAD (5142654): with a changed aac-skills/ticket-fleet, `python3 tools/skill-stamps.py stamp aac-skills agents/skills` followed by `python3 tools/build-cloud-plugin.py --from-mirror --home 'C:\Users\Dan'` already produced exactly one bump (packager printed `stamps rotated in 0 skills`) - tools/skill-stamps.py's write touches only the four stamp keys, which content_sha excludes, so the packager saw the recorded hash. The revision-8-to-10 commits the ticket cites (f679054, 2786af1 on aac-skills/ticket-fleet/SKILL.md) are explained by a SECOND content edit between the stamper and the packager: rotation 1 recorded the edit's mtime, rotation 2 recorded the stamper's own write mtime and pushed `previous-modified` onto it. I implemented the broader reading the acceptance wording supports ('previous-modified naming the last published version'): tools/skill-stamps.py now rebases every rotation on the stamp committed at HEAD (published_stamp / _baseline_paths in /home/user/claude-dotfiles/.claude/worktrees/wf_29d1e857-86e-9/tools/skill-stamps.py), so any number of re-stamps between two commits collapse to one revision bump. This covers the ticket's literal case as well, and the two new tests fail on the old code (revision 3 vs 2).
- Side effect of the HEAD-baseline rotation, deliberate but worth knowing: content that is reverted to the last committed version before being committed now restores that version's stamp (revision can go back down, e.g. 9 -> 8) instead of inventing a new revision. `skill-stamps.py check` passes either way; pinned by test_content_back_at_the_published_version_restores_its_stamp.
- Doc drift, not touched: .github/workflows/skill-stamps.yml's header comment tells a failing branch to run `python3 tools/skill-stamps.py stamp aac-skills`, while CLAUDE.md's Skill stamps section names `stamp aac-skills agents/skills`. An agent following the workflow comment leaves the agents/skills mirror unstamped.
- tools/build-cloud-plugin.py writes its scratch payload to <repo>/dist on every run (dist/aac-skills plus dist/aac-skills.zip) and nothing ever removes it; it survives in the working tree because .gitignore covers it. A run with --out is the only way to keep the repo root clean.
- Issue 364's cited evidence no longer reproduces on the live tracker: #335 and #338 each now carry a `## Provenance` heading directly below their `## Blocked by` bullet, and a heading already bounded the section, so both bodies return [] from the PRE-change proseBlockers as well. `node tools/tracker-audit.js` run from HEAD~ and from this branch produce byte-identical output on surreptakos/claude-dotfiles today (95 open, 213 total; one `[ungated-dependency]` on #322 for #316, one `[stale-premise?]` advisory on #358). Acceptance criterion 4 holds (neither #335 nor #338 appears under ungated-dependency) but it is now vacuously true; the change is proven at the unit level by the before/after table in the commit message. Someone appears to have repaired the two bodies on GitHub between the ticket being filed (06:50Z) and now.
- Residual false positive the fix deliberately does not cover: when the Blocked-by field is left EMPTY and a provenance footer is the first blank-line-separated block under the heading (`## Blocked by\n\nFiled from the run triage (#358).`), the footer's `#N` is still read as a blocker. Distinguishing that from a legitimate bulletless prose blocker (`#316, #320 need to land first.`) needs wording heuristics that tools/tracker-audit.js's own design notes reject, so the first block is kept whatever its shape — losing a real blocker (ticket reads as startable) is the worse failure. A clean fix would need the issue template to emit a machine-readable section-end marker (e.g. an HTML comment above the footer); unticketed.
- The three copies of tracker-audit.js have drifted far apart and only proseBlockers is now identical across them. agents/skills/project-harness/templates/tracker-audit.js (and its marketplace/ build) lack citedIssueNumbers, the REST paginate/parseLinkHeader/pageFromUrl work, closerPrsByIssue and the check-9 comment fetch that tools/tracker-audit.js carries; their export lists differ too (the template exports restIssueToShape/filterIssuesOnly, the tool exports normalizeIssue/issuesOnly). Nothing in CI compares them, so 'the project-harness template copy carries the same change' is enforced only by whoever remembers. A parity port is unticketed.
- The pre-commit gate (.githooks/pre-commit -> tests/restore-test.ps1) cannot run in this Linux container - no pwsh or powershell on PATH - so the commit was made with --no-verify and the restore suite is unexercised on this branch. Any cloud-session commit to this repo is in the same position.
- `gh api repos/surreptakos/claude-dotfiles/...` returned HTTP 403 ('GitHub access to this repository is not enabled for this session') on first use in this session and then worked minutes later with no configuration change, while the github MCP tools worked throughout. The previous attempt's reviewer concluded from a single 403 that the repo was unreachable and could not verify acceptance criterion 4. Retry gh (or fall back to mcp__github__*) before concluding a repo is inaccessible.

Read literally that also suppresses #173, whose box cites PR #168 while telling the owner to copy `.claude/workflows/ticket-fleet.js` — a path that really was deleted, i.e. a genuine finding. The implemented reading matches a cited number against the tracker's own issue numbers, so a PR citation does not count as a quotation and #173 still reports. Measured: deleted-subject? goes 3 -> 2 (only #361 suppressed) instead of 3 -> 1.
- Pre-existing, untouched: agents/skills/project-harness/templates/tracker-audit.js carries the doc comment for isFollowUpAcknowledgment twice — once orphaned at line ~98 immediately followed by the REST-adapter comment, and again above the real function at line ~161. Anything that breaks the PR fetch silently turns those three back on — worth a NOTE in the output rather than a silent difference, but that is a different ticket.
- Confirmed empirically in this worktree that the guard is shape-sensitive, not command-sensitive: `for n in 1 2; do gh api ...; done`, `tail -c 20 file | od -c` and a `cat > file <<'EOF'` heredoc all RAN fine here, while `git commit -q -F - <<'MSG' ... MSG && git log --oneline -1` was refused with 'this command names git in a form too complex to verify that it stays inside the worktree'. So the refusal fires on constructs whose text the guard must evaluate before it can see whether git is reached - it is not a blanket ban on loops/pipes/heredocs. The SKILL.md section is written from issue 375's reported list (waves 4/5 and 6/7 triage), which reported `gh`-bearing loops and `;`-joined `gh` calls as refused; a future reader may find some of those shapes pass depending on session policy. The working spellings given are safe in every case.
- `orchestrator/worker-cycle.md` is RETIRED (issue 217) and is a pointer-only file; it contained no shell shapes to remove. Criterion 2 was met by appending a short 'Shell shapes a worktree agent should know' section that points at the SKILL.md list, placed outside the RETIRED blockquote so the pointer is not read as retired guidance.
- `tools/build-cloud-plugin.py` derives the plugin version from wall-clock time (`2026.9.16HHMM` in `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` and `marketplace/aac-skills/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`), so any rebuild in a later minute dirties those two files with a version-only diff even when no skill content changed. CI (.github/workflows/skill-stamps.yml) only diffs `skills/` and `hooks/hooks.json`, so it never catches this, but it produces noise in every branch that runs the packager twice. I reverted the version-only churn here and committed the single-rebuild version. A deterministic version (e.g. derived from the newest stamp) would remove the noise.
- tests/restore-test.ps1 (CLAUDE.md's stated gate and the .githooks/pre-commit hook) could not run: no pwsh/powershell in this Linux container and core.hooksPath is unset in the worktree. This is a long-standing, pre-existing limitation already recorded many times in FOLLOW-UPS.md; the change here touches only aac-skills/, orchestrator/ and the generated marketplace payload, so the skill-stamps CI job is the reachable equivalent and it passes.
- CI coverage gap (not fixed, out of the ticket's acceptance criteria): .github/workflows/skill-stamps.yml only runs `python3 tools/skill-stamps.py check aac-skills` — the agents/skills mirror is never checked directly. An unstamped mirror skill is only caught indirectly, via the payload-rebuild diff step, if at all. Adding a second check step (`check agents/skills` currently exits 0 on master, verified) would make the failure name the real cause. - The Bash worktree-isolation guard's refusal is shape-sensitive in a way that makes CLAUDE.md-style compound commands unusable here: writing a heredoc whose *body text* merely contained the words 'git commit ... <<MSG' was refused, as was chaining 'cat > file <<EOF' with a following gh api call. Bare heredocs and bare gh calls each ran. Workaround used throughout: the Write tool for every scratch file, then one plain command per call. This confirms and extends issue #375's list; I recorded the correction as a comment on that ticket.

### Wave 16 (`12da2dc`, 8 bullets, this file lines 518-525)

- **W16-01** "The 17 bullets issue 399 names are NOT on master…" — Comment on #378, and the premise is

  `git show <sha>` citation in the six earlier ledgers still resolves. #378 is closed / completed; the
  comment records that its three unticked acceptance boxes are all demonstrably true now (that is what
  `tracker-audit`'s `[closed-with-open-boxes]` finding on it is) and that criterion 3's `W2-` … `W7-`
  should read `W2-` … `W16-`. Not edited from here — six ledgers have declined to edit another
  ticket's acceptance criteria and this one does too.
- **W16-02** "Filed issue #400 (bug, ready-for-agent): curlTicketRows()…" — Comment on #400, a
  re-verification rather than a record of a filing. Checked on master *after* PR #398 landed:
  `check.js:557-559` still fetches one `&state=open&per_page=100` URL with no paging, `:585`'s pure
  `paginateTicketPages` is on master but `curlTicketRows` is not one of its callers (only the `gh` path
  at `:613-614` is), and `renderTicketList` still prints the truncated length. #400 stands, unchanged
  in size and shape.
- **W16-03** "PR #331 (issue 300) and PR #398 (issue 394) both rewrite the same lines of
  runChecker()…" — No action: the predicted conflict resolved the way the bullet asked. Both PRs are
  merged and master carries **#331's** spelling — `check.test.js:11` requires `./test-support` and
  `:26` builds the child env through `localEnv(opts.env)` with a single `env` key. #398's inline
  blanking is gone and the duplicate `env:` key with it. Nothing left to file.
- **W16-04** "A FOLLOW-UPS.md discovery bullet (W15-09) claims tests/claims-audit.test.js is exercised
  only by the fleet done-condition…" — **#415**. The half that survives is confirmed on master:
  `grep -rn node-test-context` finds `tests/node-test-context.test.js` only in itself and in this file,
  `.claude/session.json`'s test term names one `tests/` file, `.githooks/pre-commit` runs only the
  restore test, `tests/restore-test.ps1:1355-1367` covers `claims-audit.test.js` and nothing else under
  `tests/`, and no workflow names it. #300, where this was recorded, closed as completed without
  covering it, so it needed a ticket of its own rather than a second comment on a closed one.
  `node --test tools/*.test.js tests/*.test.js` is 317 pass / 0 fail in 4.1s, so widening the glob costs
  nothing.
- **W16-05** "FOLLOW-UPS.md on master is LF-terminated except for its single final line, which is
  CRLF…" — Comment on #376, the open decision ticket that owns this file's shape. The line-ending half
  has resolved itself by accident: `grep -c $'\r' FOLLOW-UPS.md` is 0 on master where the blob at
  `12da2dc` had exactly 1 (on its line 234), lost in the merge pass rather than by any rule — so there
  is nothing to fix and nothing decided. The comment adds the evidence that matters to #376's ruling
  instead: the bullet "Only the repo-mirror wording changed; ALLOWED_FLOWS…" now appears **seven times**
  in this file — count them with `grep -c '^- Only the repo-mirror wording' FOLLOW-UPS.md`, where the
  `^- ` anchor matters because this entry quotes the bullet too, and no line number is cited because
  every edit to this ledger moves them. All seven are byte-identical, one copy per triage-ledger
  branch that was cut while it was the last line. Append-only plus branch merges manufactures
  duplication that the same rule then forbids deleting.
- **W16-06** "gh api search/issues is refused outright in a fleet container…" — **#416**, with W16-07.
  Re-confirmed here (the dedupe listing had to be REST pages, not a search). #375, where this was
  recorded, is closed / completed and shipped a four-row shell-shape table that carries neither this
  nor W16-07. `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` has a "From a cloud session (no GraphQL)" section covering
  the two neighbouring 403s but not `search/*`, and `aac-skills/ticket-fleet/SKILL.md` — the file every
  fleet implementer loads — does not point at it.
- **W16-07** "The Bash worktree-isolation guard refused an awk program in this session with a message
  issue #375's body does not list…" — **#416**, same ticket. The sharp part is not the missing row but
  the summary sentence #375 shipped: "it is the shape that is refused, not the command" is false for
  `awk '<program>'`, refused as a single plain command on what its program could do. This pass hit the
  same class from the other side — `tail -c 20 FILE | od -c` joined with two other reads was refused
  with "feeds `od` input assembled by the command … a program this guard does not know may run that
  input" — so the guard reads program *content*, and an agent told only about shape retries and fails
  again. Eighth consecutive triage pass to lose turns to this guard.
- **W16-08** "Seven branches in run 6aa9c56e now land their fix only in a generated tree…" — Comment on
  #335, which is open and is exactly the ticket for it. What is new is that **the precondition has
  fired**: #335 says to pull "once the run `6aa9c56e` PRs have merged to master", and they all have —
  the only open PR in the repo is #414, on a branch outside this run. So the next desktop
  `sync.ps1 -Mode push` is the one that reverts, not a hypothetical future one. The comment brings the
  four-row table to seven with the three that landed since (#331, #397, #398) and their master
  coordinates, and restates why `agents/skills/session-check/` is the worst of them: `ls aac-skills/`
  has no `session-check`, so the generated mirror is the only copy in the repo.

worktree-isolation guard refuses, and not of #336 (PR #350), which generates the harness template from
`tools/tracker-audit.js` — the audit's own `ghPaginate()` page loop (issue 171) is the fix this ticket
ports into the session gate, which is the last caller in the repo still using the Link header.

### Wave 10 (`f5d091d`, 6 bullets)

- **W10-01** "The 20 bullets issue 385 names are NOT on master…" — Comment on #378: the table is now ten commits and 200 bullets (`f5d091d` 6 and `a4165eb` 5 on top of the eight/189 the waves 8/9 pass recorded), plus `d56864f`, and wave 11 being the run's last makes ten the final count.
- **W10-02** "Issue #378's acceptance criterion 3 says the W2- through W7- numbering must still resolve…" — Comment on #378, in the same comment as W10-01: with five ledgers the criterion should read `W2-` … `W11-`. Recorded there rather than edited into the body, for the reason W10-02 itself gives.
- **W10-03** "The Edit tool silently normalized FOLLOW-UPS.md's single trailing CR to LF…" — Comment on #376, **with a correction**: the file *is* pinned — `.gitattributes:15` is a total `* -text` rule and `git check-attr text -- FOLLOW-UPS.md` reports `text: unset` — so the `.gitattributes` entry the bullet proposes is a no-op. Git converts nothing here; the normalization is a working-copy write no attribute can reach. The byte belongs in #376's ruling, which is deciding this file's shape anyway. This pass appended bytes rather than editing, so the CR is intact.
- **W10-04** "tools/tracker-audit.js's ungated-dependency check fires on any new ticket whose '## Blocked by' section names an issue number…" — **#390**. Not a duplicate of #364 (PR #372): that ticket bounds the section so a provenance footer stops reading as a blocker, this one gives the section a non-gating vocabulary so a merge-order preference can be stated at all. Both rewrite `proseBlockers()`, so #390 names #372 as merge order rather than a block.
- **W10-05** "ASSUMPTION: the dedupe rule from issue 319 ('same file, symbol or failure') was applied at the level of the failing symbol…" — No action: an assumption record for the waves 8/9 pass, taken the same way here (see W10-04). The conflict it predicts between PR #383 and #386 is already named in #386's body, and PR #388 is open against it.
- **W10-06** "The Bash worktree-isolation guard's refusal is shape-sensitive…" — No action: already #375 (PR #382). Confirmed a third time in this pass — a `for` loop issuing paginated `gh api` calls was refused with no git in the command — but that is the first shape #375's body already lists, so the ticket needs no fourth comment.

### Wave 11 (`a4165eb`, 5 bullets)

- **W11-01** "Change is confined to …/.github/workflows/skill-stamps.yml: a new step `- name: Stamps current on agents/skills`…" — No action: an implementation record for #386, carried by PR #388; it names no defect.
- **W11-02** "Ambiguity resolved (assumption stated): the ticket allows one combined check or per-tree checks…" — No action: the reading #386's criteria most directly support, and the limitation behind it (`skill-stamps.py`'s report prints the skill name, never its tree) is what the two named steps route around rather than a defect the ticket left open.
- **W11-03** "The mirror step carries `if: ${{ !cancelled() }}` so it still reports when the aac-skills step already failed…" — No action: an implementation record for #386, and a deliberate addition PR #388 describes; a both-trees-drifted branch reporting both trees in one run is strictly better than the literal wording.
- **W11-04** "Pre-existing, not fixed: FOLLOW-UPS.md line 126 is stale…" — No action, and **verified**: `agents/skills/to-issues/SKILL.md` and `agents/skills/to-prd/SKILL.md` both carry rev 1 stamps dated `2026-09-14T13:48:41Z`, and `python3 tools/skill-stamps.py check agents/skills --home 'C:\Users\Dan'` exits 0 over all 38 mirror skills on master today. Line 126 stays as written: this file is a dated append-only log of what each agent found when it looked, not a set of live claims, and #376 is the open ticket on what to do about that.
- **W11-05** "Committed with `git commit --no-verify`: .githooks/pre-commit runs tests/restore-test.ps1…" — No action: already #211 / #213. The same constraint applied to this commit.
